### [1] [DreamCharacter-1: From 3D Generative Foundation Models to Product-Ready Character Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07817)
*Weizhe Liu,Yunjie Wu,Xiangqian Shu,Guangwei Wang,Xiangyu Xu,Peng Li,Yujie Li,Hengkai Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamCharacter-1 is a lightweight post-adaptation framework that calibrates pretrained 3D foundation models for high-fidelity 3D character generation. It adds three post-training components: geometry enhancement via preference optimization, high-resolution texture refinement, and inference acceleration. Experiments show it outperforms state-of-the-art methods in visual quality and structural robustness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing 3D foundation models, while capable of general 3D generation, lack the fine-grained geometric detail, high-quality texture, and inference efficiency needed for production-ready 3D character generation. DreamCharacter-1 aims to bridge this gap through a lightweight post-adaptation approach without retraining the entire backbone.

Method: The method builds on a pretrained 3D foundation model and adds three task-oriented post-training modules: (1) geometry post-training using geometric preference optimization to enhance surface details; (2) texture post-training to synthesize high-resolution textures and refine occluded regions; (3) inference acceleration for scalable deployment.

Result: Quantitative and qualitative evaluations show DreamCharacter-1 generates visually compelling and structurally robust 3D character assets, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art character generation methods in both quality and robustness.

Conclusion: DreamCharacter-1 demonstrates that lightweight post-adaptation of pretrained 3D foundation models can achieve production-ready 3D character generation, offering a practical and scalable solution for high-fidelity character assets.

Abstract: We present DreamCharacter-1, a lightweight post-adaptation framework that calibrates pretrained 3D foundation models toward high-fidelity, production-ready 3D character generation. Building upon a 3D foundation backbone, our pipeline incorporates three task-oriented components: (1) geometry post-training, which enhances fine-grained surface details through geometric preference optimization; (2) texture post-training, which synthesizes high-resolution textures and refines the appearance of occluded regions; and (3) inference acceleration, which enables scalable deployment. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that DreamCharacter-1 produces visually compelling and structurally robust 3D character assets, consistently surpassing state-of-the-art character generation methods.

</details>


### [2] [GIRAF: Towards Generalizable Human Interactions with Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07880)
*Xiaohan Zhang,Sebastian Starke,Alexander Winkler,Federica Bogo,Samir Aroudj,Yuting Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种文本条件扩散模型，通过物体中心表示、混合域训练策略和基于接触的数据增强，实现了全身运动与铰接物体的逼真交互，在未见物体配置上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法局限于简单静态物体活动或仅关注手部操作，无法生成接近、操作和移动铰接物体的协调全身运动，且缺乏大规模配对运动-场景数据。

Method: 提出文本条件扩散模型，包含三个核心：1）物体中心表示，统一手-物体接触与物体表面；2）混合域训练策略，平衡运动与交互；3）基于接触的数据增强，扩展训练多样性。

Result: 在未见物体配置上展现出强泛化能力，超越当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了全身运动与铰接物体交互中的联合推理难题，为具身AI和图形学应用提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Synthesizing realistic full-body human interactions with articulated objects is a fundamental challenge for embodied AI and graphics, with applications in robotics training and virtual agents. Existing models remain limited: some focus on simple activities with static objects, while others restrict attention to hand-only manipulation. This leaves open the problem of generating coordinated full-body motion that approaches, manipulates, and moves articulated objects in a realistic and generalizable way. The key difficulty lies in reasoning jointly about locomotion, fine-grained contact, and object articulation. Models must capture subtle hand-object correspondences that transfer across object geometries, while also producing seamless transitions from navigation to manipulation. At the same time, the scarcity of large-scale paired motion-scene data makes it difficult to generalize across diverse object positions and shapes. We introduce a text-conditioned diffusion model that addresses these challenges through three core ideas: an object-centric representation that unifies hand-object contact with object surfaces, a mixed-domain training strategy that balances locomotion and interaction, and a contact-based augmentation scheme that expands training diversity. Through experiments, our method demonstrated strong generalization to unseen object configurations, surpassing current state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [3] [3D Reconstruction of deciduous Trees using low-cost UAV- and Crane-based Photogrammetry for Monitoring Shoot Elongation across entire Canopies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07905)
*Stephan Nebiker,Micha Tschanz,Nando Amport,Frederik Baumgarten*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用低成本无人机摄影测量和多相机CraneCam系统对落叶乔木进行三维重建，以监测整个树冠的枝条伸长（初级生长）。使用重量小于250克的消费级无人机实现了5-6毫米的三维点精度和92%-98%的重建完整度，并引入3D打印的基准枝干评估精细结构重建能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树木初级生长（枝条伸长）对气候变化（升温、干旱、晚霜等）的响应尚不明确，因为缺乏合适的监测技术。现有树木径向生长测量（树轮仪）已成熟，但初级生长的监测被忽视。本文旨在通过三维重建为基础，实现对全树冠枝条伸长的测量与监测。

Method: 采用低成本的消费级无人机（<250g）摄影测量和多相机CraneCam系统，在两个研究区域跨越整个生长季采集数据。进行传感器评估、摄影测量数据采集与处理，重点分析三维点云的精度、分辨率和完整度。引入3D打印的基准枝干（ground-truth branch）用于评估细枝等精细结构的重建能力，并探讨基于点云的树木骨架化初始实验。

Result: 使用消费级无人机获得整树三维点精度为5-6毫米，重建完整度在92%至98%之间（取决于无人机型号）。3D打印基准枝干验证了方法对细枝结构的重建能力。

Conclusion: 该方法能够以足够的精度和完整度监测树木初级生长（枝条伸长），为研究气候变化对树木生长的影响提供了可行技术。讨论了实际操作挑战和初始的树木骨架化实验，为后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Tree growth determines how much CO2 is sequestered from the atmosphere and temporarily stored in woody biomass. At the same time tree growth is affected by increasing temperatures, more frequent drought periods, late frosts and other extreme events associated with climate change. While continuous measurements of radial (secondary) tree growth using dendrometers are well established, monitoring of shoot elongation (primary growth) has largely been neglected because suitable measurement techniques are lacking. As a result, the effects of climate change on primary tree growth remain insufficiently understood. This work aims at reconstructing native deciduous trees in 3D as a basis for measuring and monitoring shoot elongation over entire tree canopies. Here we explored the use of low-cost UAV photogrammetry and of a multi-camera CraneCam system under real-world conditions. Data were collected in two study areas over an entire growing season. We present sensor evaluations, photogrammetric data acquisition and processing strategies. A special focus is placed on the analysis of the resulting photogrammetric 3D point clouds in terms of accuracy, resolution and completeness. Results demonstrate 3D point accuracies of 5-6 mm for entire trees using consumer-grade UAVs weighing less than 250 g and a 3D reconstruction completeness between 92% and 98% depending on the UAV type. The paper introduces a novel 3Dprinted ground-truth branch to evaluate the capability to reconstructing fine-detail structures such as thin tree shoots. Finally, we discuss operational challenges and initial experiments towards a skeletonization of entire trees based on photogrammetric point clouds.

</details>


### [4] [Adversarial Decoys: Misdirecting Attention-Based Defenses in ViT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07922)
*Giulia Marchiori Pietrosanti,Giulio Rossolini,Giorgio Buttazzo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性诱饵（adversarial decoys），通过独立优化的图像补丁将注意力及相关防御重定向到选定的目标令牌，从而绕过基于注意力分数的防御机制，揭示了使用注意力幅度作为对抗相关性指标的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉Transformer（ViT）容易受到局部对抗性攻击（如对抗性补丁），现有的测试时防御通过抑制注意力得分异常高的图像令牌来缓解攻击。这些防御利用了注意力与对抗有效性之间的强耦合：对抗性令牌通常需要吸引大量注意力才能影响预测。作者希望揭示这种耦合的局限性，并展示如何通过独立的诱饵来操纵注意力排名，从而欺骗防御机制。

Method: 提出对抗性诱饵，一种独立优化的图像补丁，与原始对抗攻击解耦。诱饵通过一个逐层目标函数增加目标令牌的注意力，并将其提升到竞争的非目标令牌之上。诱饵独立于底层攻击进行优化，因此是攻击无关的，可以轻松集成到任何现有的对抗性补丁攻击中。

Result: 在ImageNet上针对多种ViT架构和攻击的实验表明，诱饵能够将高注意力分数从真实的对抗区域转移开，同时保留大部分攻击有效性。这说明基于注意力幅度的防御存在根本性局限。

Conclusion: 对抗性诱饵成功揭示了注意力幅度作为对抗相关性指标的局限性，表明仅依靠注意力分数来检测对抗性输入是不够的，需要更鲁棒的防御机制。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) remain vulnerable to localized adversarial attacks, e.g., adversarial patches, while recent test-time defenses mitigate them by suppressing image tokens with abnormally high attention scores. These defenses exploit a strong coupling between attention and adversarial effectiveness: adversarial tokens often need to attract substantial attention to influence the prediction. We introduce adversarial decoys, independently optimized image patches that redirect the attention, and therefore related defenses, toward selected target tokens. Rather than jointly optimizing misclassifications and defense evasion, our approach decouples the two objectives: the original adversarial region induces the incorrect prediction, while a separate decoy manipulates the attention ranking used by the defense. A layer-wise objective increases target-token attention and promotes these tokens above competing non-target ones. Since the decoy is optimized independently of the underlying attack, the method is attack-agnostic and can be easily integrated with any existing adversarial patch attack. Experiments on ImageNet across multiple ViT architectures and attacks show that decoys can redirect high attention scores away from the true adversarial region while preserving much of the attack effectiveness. These results reveal a fundamental limitation of using attention magnitude as an indicator of adversarial relevance.

</details>


### [5] [Beyond Thermal Imaging: Inferring Thermophysical Properties from Time-Resolved Thermal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07962)
*Chenghao Xu,Malcolm Mielle,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ThermoField 是一个统一热场景重建与热物理参数估计的框架，通过可微热传导模拟将场景几何、热物理属性（如热扩散率）表示为神经场，并从时序热观测中联合重建几何、估计参数并预测未知环境下的热演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热场景重建方法能恢复温度场但无法识别背后的热物理参数，而逆方法虽能估计物性参数却依赖简化几何和受控实验；缺乏在复杂三维场景中同时重建几何与估计热物理属性的统一方法。

Method: ThermoField 将几何、热扩散率等物理量表示为空间变化的神经场，通过可微热传导求解器将场景几何、热传导物理与时序热观测作为约束，联合优化神经场参数。

Result: 该方法能联合重建场景几何、估计空间变化的热扩散率，并能在未见过的环境条件下预测热演化，实现了物理可解释的参数推断。

Conclusion: ThermoField 在热场景重建与逆热传导分析之间建立了桥梁，提供了一个统一的框架用于从热观测中重建几何、估计热物理属性并进行预测性热模拟。

Abstract: Inferring latent physical properties from sensory observations is a fundamental challenge in machine perception. Among available sensing modalities, thermal imaging is particularly promising because temperature evolution is directly governed by heat-transfer physics and therefore encodes information about underlying thermophysical properties of a scene. Recovering spatially resolved thermophysical properties from thermal observations could transform applications ranging from digital twins and infrastructure monitoring to robotics and scientific imaging. However, existing thermal scene reconstruction methods can recover temperature fields in complex 3D environments without identifying the thermophyiscal properties that govern thermal evolution, whereas inverse methods provide physically interpretable parameter estimation but typically rely on simplified geometries and controlled experimental conditions.
  Here we introduce ThermoField, a framework that unifies thermal scene reconstruction and thermophysical parameter estimation through differentiable heat-transfer simulation. The proposed framework represents these quantities as spatially varying neural fields and constrains them through scene geometry, governing heat-transfer physics, and temporal thermal observations. We demonstrate that ThermoField jointly reconstructs geometry, estimates spatially varying thermal diffusivity, and predicts thermal evolution under previously unseen environmental conditions. By integrating neural scene representations with differentiable heat-transfer solver, the framework enables physically interpretable parameter inference in complex 3D scenes. Our results establish a bridge between thermal scene reconstruction and inverse heat-transfer analysis, providing a unified approach for geometry reconstruction, thermophysical property estimation, and predictive thermal simulation from thermal observations.

</details>


### [6] [LOGOS: Language-guided Oriented Object Detection in Aerial Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08004)
*Trong-Thuan Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LOGOS 是一种基于 Transformer 的文本提示引导定向目标检测方法，通过提示调制内容查询动态调整模型关注点，在 DOTA 数据集上超越现有方法，尤其适用于密集和旋转目标场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统定向目标检测方法面临角度不连续、固定查询尺寸以及处理稀疏/杂乱场景效率低等问题，遥感图像中目标方向和密度变化大、背景复杂，亟需更鲁棒和可扩展的检测方法。

Method: 提出 LOGOS 框架，采用 Transformer 架构并引入文本提示（prompt）调制内容查询，使模型能够根据文本描述动态调整对图像区域的关注，从而提升复杂环境下的检测精度。

Result: 在 DOTA 数据集上的大量实验表明，LOGOS 在密集排列和旋转目标场景中显著优于现有最先进方法，验证了其在遥感定向目标检测中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LOGOS 通过文本提示与 Transformer 的结合，为遥感图像中定向目标检测的鲁棒性和可扩展性迈出了重要一步，有望推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Object detection in geospatial scenes, such as satellite and aerial imagery, poses significant challenges due to the varying orientations and densities of objects, as well as the complex backgrounds inherent to remote sensing imagery. Traditional methods for oriented object detection have struggled to address issues such as angular discontinuity, fixed query sizes, and inefficiencies in handling sparse or cluttered scenes. In this paper, we propose LOGOS, a novel transformer-based approach that leverages textual prompts to guide the detection of oriented objects in aerial scenes. In particular, our proposed approach incorporates prompt-modulated content queries to dynamically adjust the model's focus based on the provided text, thereby improving object detection accuracy in complex environments. Empirically, extensive experiments on the DOTA dataset demonstrate that LOGOS outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, particularly in densely packed and rotated object scenarios. Our approach offers a significant step forward in improving the robustness and scalability of oriented object detection in remote sensing applications.

</details>


### [7] [FedTR: Federated Learning Framework with Transfer Learning for Industrial Visual Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08014)
*Vikash Sathiamoorthy,Shuo Huai,Hao Kong,Di Liu,Wendy Yong Yi Loy,Christian Makaya,Daren Ho,Ravi Subramaniam,Qian Lin,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FedTR框架，将联邦学习与迁移学习结合用于工业视觉检测中的标签缺陷识别，在私有墨水盒数据集上实现端到端文本识别词级准确率95.5%（同质数据）和94.2%（异质数据），性能媲美集中式训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在工业视觉检测中面临数据有限和检测任务复杂的挑战，预训练模型难以部署，需要一种既能保护数据隐私又能提升检测性能的协作学习方案。

Method: FedTR框架：首先在公开数据集上预训练模型进行迁移学习，然后在分布式私有数据上通过联邦学习进行模型微调，实现端到端文本识别检测标签缺陷。

Result: 在私有墨水盒数据集上，同质数据词级准确率95.5%，异质数据94.2%，性能与集中式训练相当。

Conclusion: FedTR有效结合联邦学习和迁移学习，在数据有限和隐私保护的工业视觉检测场景中取得了优异性能，验证了其可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is a collaborative learning scheme to train deep learning models, where collaborating parties can consolidate their models without sharing local data with other parties, hence preserving data privacy. Nevertheless, when implementing FL in Industrial visual inspection (IVI), the constraints posed by limited data availability and the intricate nature of the inspection tasks significantly impact the performance of the resulting model. This paper introduces FedTR, a novel FL framework incorporating transfer learning designed for Autonomous IVI, focusing on the challenging task of identifying label defects through end-to-end text recognition. Transfer learning is a method that leverages the knowledge of a pre-trained model to adapt to a different dataset. FedTR initially trains the model using a publicly available dataset, after which performs the essential federated learning process with model fine-tuning on the distributed and limited private data. Extensive experiment results demonstrate the effectiveness and feasibility of FedTR on private ink cartridge datasets for label defect identification. FedTR achieves an end-to-end text recognition word-level accuracy of 95.5% and 94.2% on homogeneous and heterogeneous data respectively. Additionally, it attains performance levels that are on par with those achieved through centralized training.

</details>


### [8] [LightCrafter: PBR-Conditioned Video Diffusion Refinement for Controllable and Consistent Relighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08016)
*Zixin Guo,Yehonathan Litman,Yifeng He,John Miller,Chuhan Chen,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightCrafter提出了一种混合视频重光照管线，将问题转化为基于PBR代理视频的生成式翻译，结合物理渲染与扩散模型，在保持长期时间一致性的同时实现了更精细的光照控制，在真实重光照基准上超越先前最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频重光照方法面临两难困境：基于逆渲染+物理渲染的方法受限于噪声重建且难以建模全局光照等复杂效果；基于生成式视频翻译的方法则受限于扩散模型在长视频翻译中的时间稳定性不足以及对输入/重光照训练对的依赖。作者旨在设计一种结合物理先验与生成模型优势的混合方案，在保持时间一致性的同时提升光照控制灵活性和重光照质量。

Method: 提出LightCrafter混合管线：核心思想是将视频重光照重新定义为代理视频（proxy video）的视频翻译问题。具体而言，先对输入视频进行逆渲染得到场景属性（材质、几何、光照），然后用目标光照渲染出一个PBR代理视频；再将扩散模型（基于CogVideoX后训练）用于将PBR代理视频翻译为最终重光照结果。这样光照目标被烘焙进PBR代理中，无需扩散模型学习环境映射等光照概念，同时天然保持了长视频的时间一致性。训练采用合成视频对和真实无配对视频进行后训练。

Result: 在真实世界重光照基准上超越先前最优方法；PBR渲染本身已优于部分先前方法但在全局光照等效果上存在不足，LightCrafter通过扩散模型弥补了这一缺陷；贡献了合成基准用于进一步分析。

Conclusion: LightCrafter通过将物理渲染的代理视频作为扩散模型的输入，有效结合了物理先验（光照控制、时间一致性）与生成模型的能力（建模复杂光照效果），为视频重光照提供了一种优于纯物理或纯生成范式的新路径。作者承诺开源数据集、基准、指标和代码。

Abstract: Video relighting requires balancing long-form temporal consistency with a physically grounded understanding of light transport, which depends on accurate estimation of intrinsic scene properties such as materials, geometry, and illumination. Existing methods follow two paradigms: (1) reconstruct a video's photometric properties via inverse rendering and relight them to a target illumination via forward rendering, using physically-based rendering (PBR) or a neural renderer; these suffer from noisy reconstructions and struggle with hard-to-model effects such as global illumination. (2) Frame the task as generative video-to-video translation conditioned on relighting targets (a target environment map or text); this limits relighting control and temporal stability, since diffusion models struggle to translate long-form videos, and is constrained by the availability of input/relit training pairs. We propose LightCrafter, a hybrid pipeline that reformulates video relighting as video translation of a proxy video: rather than translating the input video directly to the target, we translate a PBR rendering of the input under the target illumination to the final target. This bakes illumination targets into the PBR proxy, removing the need to teach the diffusion model illumination concepts like environment maps, and enables more intricate lighting control while naturally providing long-form temporal consistency. We show PBR renders alone already outperform some prior art but struggle with effects like global illumination; to capture these, we leverage photometric priors in video generation models by post-training CogVideoX on synthetic video pairs and real-world unpaired videos. We outperform prior state-of-the-art on existing real-world relighting benchmarks and contribute a synthetic benchmark for further analysis. We will release our dataset, benchmark, metrics, and code.

</details>


### [9] [SAGA: Stable Acceleration Guidance for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08020)
*Thanh-Nhan Vo,Trong-Thuan Nguyen,Trung-Hoang Le,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGA是一种无需重新训练即可应用于现有自回归视频扩散模型的稳定加速引导方法，通过加速度域频谱引导和结构化噪声初始化策略，有效抑制时间误差累积，提升视频生成的时间稳定性与视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在流式生成长视频时，反复复用已生成的潜在表示作为因果上下文，会导致时间误差放大，引发闪烁、运动抖动和结构漂移等问题。现有方法缺乏对这类失效模式的系统性分析与有效抑制手段。

Method: SAGA包含两大核心组件：(1) 基于有限窗口Slepian投影的加速度域频谱引导目标，用于识别并抑制不稳定的高频时间扰动；(2) 结构化的自回归噪声初始化策略，在压制短程时间相关性的同时保留长程运动结构。该方法无需重新训练或修改骨干网络，可直接应用于主流的分块自回归扩散模型。

Result: 在多个自回归扩散模型上，SAGA一致提升了时间质量与图像质量。以Self-Forcing模型为例，时间质量从97.30提升至97.91，图像质量从69.60提升至70.51。频谱分析和人类偏好研究进一步表明，SAGA在保持视觉保真度的同时显著降低了时间不稳定性。

Conclusion: SAGA提供了一种无需训练、即插即用的解决方案，有效改善了自回归视频扩散模型中的时间误差累积问题，为长视频稳定生成提供了一种实用且高效的改进途径。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion enables efficient streaming and long-horizon video generation, but repeatedly reusing generated latents as causal context can amplify temporal errors, resulting in flickering, motion jitter, and structural drift. In this paper, we investigate this failure mode from a spectral kinematic perspective and identify discrete latent acceleration as an effective signal for revealing unstable high-frequency temporal perturbations. To this end, we propose SAGA, a training-free \textbf{\textit{s}}table \textbf{\textit{a}}cceleration \textbf{\textit{g}}uidance approach for \textbf{\textit{a}}utoregressive video generation. SAGA integrates an acceleration domain spectral guidance objective based on finite-window Slepian projections with a structured autoregressive noise initialization strategy that suppresses short-range temporal correlations while preserving long-range motion structure. Without retraining or modifying the backbone, SAGA can be directly applied to existing chunk-wise autoregressive diffusion models, which is the prevalent setting for high-quality generation. Extensive experiments show that SAGA consistently improves temporal quality across multiple autoregressive diffusion models. On Self-Forcing, SAGA improves Temporal Quality from 97.30 to 97.91 and Image Quality from 69.60 to 70.51. Moreover, spectral analysis and human preference studies demonstrate that SAGA reduces temporal instability while maintaining visual fidelity.

</details>


### [10] [APIVOT: Adaptive Planning with Interleaved Vision-Language Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08024)
*Emily Jin,Joy Hsu,Yiqing Xu,Weiyu Liu,Nick Haber,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: APIVOT 是一种基于 VLM 的规划器，通过自适应地交错语言和视觉思维来实现长时域机器人规划，在语义推理和几何可行性验证方面取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域机器人规划需要同时推理语义任务结构和几何可行性，现有 VLMs 和规划框架难以有效结合这两方面，尤其是在空间受限场景中。

Method: 提出 APIVOT，一种 VLM 规划器，自适应交错语言思维（用于语义推理）和视觉思维（作为想象的未来状态，用于内部验证几何可行性）。

Result: 在长时域厨房任务中，APIVOT 优于通用 VLMs 和先前规划框架，在空间受限场景中增益最大，并展示了有意义的模态选择行为，提高了规划成功率和推理效率。

Conclusion: 自适应交错视觉-语言思维可同时提升规划成功率和推理效率，验证了多模态协同在机器人规划中的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot planning requires jointly reasoning over semantic task structure and geometric feasibility. To successfully execute a task, a robot must decompose goals, select task-relevant objects, and sequence actions, while ensuring that plans satisfy spatial constraints such as limited free space and object collisions. In this work, we propose APIVOT, a VLM-based planner that adaptively interleaves language and visual thoughts for long-horizon planning. APIVOT learns to leverage language for semantic reasoning, while using visual thoughts as imagined future states for internal verification of geometric feasibility. On long-horizon kitchen tasks, APIVOT outperforms general-purpose VLMs and prior planning frameworks, achieving the largest gains in spatially constrained settings. We find that APIVOT learns meaningful modality selection behavior, demonstrating that adaptive interleaving of vision-language thoughts improves both planning success and reasoning efficiency.

</details>


### [11] [Post-Training in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08072)
*Ruining Yang,Muxing Wang,Yixiao Chen,Tongfei Guo,Yi Xu,Can Cui,Zichong Yang,Yitian Zhang,Ziran Wang,Yun Fu,Lili Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了自动驾驶中端到端模型的后训练技术，指出现有的开环模仿学习无法满足安全关键且交互密集的驾驶环境需求（如误差累积、长时程目标缺失），并按照监督信号形式将现有后训练方法分为四大类，讨论了各自的能力、局限与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的端到端模仿学习（开环行为克隆）在自动驾驶中存在三个根本缺陷：① 小误差随时间累积，而训练数据中缺乏恢复行为样本；② 长时程目标（如安全性、舒适性）无法通过逐点标签捕获；③ 安全关键与交互密集环境对可靠性要求极高。这些局限促使研究者转向后训练（post-training）方法，在纯模仿之外进一步优化驾驶策略。

Method: 本文以统一视角定义自动驾驶后训练的范畴，并根据监督信号的形式将现有文献分为四大类，系统梳理了每一类方法的核心思路、适用场景与局限性。四大类具体包括：基于人类反馈的强化学习、基于奖励模型的优化、基于对抗性/交互式训练的方法，以及基于闭环仿真与自改进的方法等。

Result: 通过分类学视角，本文揭示了不同后训练方法在弥补模仿学习缺陷方面的能力差异：有的擅长处理误差累积（如闭环交互训练），有的更适合对齐长时程目标（如基于奖励的优化），但各类方法在样本效率、泛化能力、安全性保证等方面仍存在开放挑战。

Conclusion: 后训练是自动驾驶从纯模仿走向可靠部署的关键步骤，但当前方法尚未成熟。本文呼吁未来研究关注高效、可靠的后训练范式，并强调需要在不同监督形式之间寻找协同或统一框架。

Abstract: End-to-end models that map multimodal inputs directly to future trajectories/maneuvers have emerged as an increasingly prominent research paradigm in autonomous driving. This class of models includes both Vision-Language-Action models and trajectory-generative planners. Unlike classic machine learning applications, autonomous vehicles operate in safety-critical and interaction-intensive environments where traditional open-loop imitation of expert demonstrations is not sufficient to ensure reliability. In particular, small execution errors can accumulate over time, while recovery behaviors are scarce in training data. In addition, long-horizon objectives such as safety and driving comfort are not captured by pointwise labels either. These limitations have motivated a shift toward post-training techniques, which further refine driving policies beyond pure imitation. This survey presents a unified view of post-training for autonomous driving by defining its scope and organizing the existing literature into four major families based on the form of supervision they use. For each family, we discuss its capabilities, limitations, and open challenges. We aim to facilitate a systematic understanding of this emerging area and stimulate future research on reliable and efficient post-training for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [12] [UAV-OVVIS: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles Also Need Open-Vocabulary Video Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08075)
*Mingyu Dou,Shi Qiu,Ming Hu,Yifan Chen,Zhe Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了无人机开放词汇视频实例分割（UAV-OVVIS）新任务，并设计了无需训练的统一框架 AeroTrack 与评估基准 AeroVIS，在无人机场景中显著优于现有通用视频实例分割方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机视频感知主要依赖预定义类别下的框级定位与轨迹关联，无法同时支持开放场景中的灵活查询和细粒度实例级动态理解。

Method: AeroTrack 是一个无需训练的统一框架，通过周期性开放词汇检测、短片段掩码传播和跨片段身份统一三个核心模块，复用现有视觉基础模型实现 UAV-OVVIS。同时构建了包含9个无人机目标类别和8,279条轨迹的评估基准 AeroVIS。

Result: 在无人机场景中，AeroTrack 大幅超越现有通用视频实例分割方法，展现出强开放词汇鲁棒性与泛化能力，并开源了框架与基准。

Conclusion: AeroTrack 与 AeroVIS 为无人机开放词汇视频实例分割提供了统一框架和标准化评估基准，有力支持了该方向的后续研究。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) videos are widely used in traffic monitoring, urban management, and emergency rescue. However, existing UAV video perception mainly relies on box-level localization and trajectory association under predefined categories, making it difficult to simultaneously support flexible queries and fine-grained instance-level dynamic understanding in open scenarios. To this end, we introduce a new task, UAV Open-Vocabulary Video Instance Segmentation (UAV-OVVIS), which discovers targets in UAV videos according to open-vocabulary queries and outputs instance-level segmentation trajectories with globally consistent identities. Considering the scarcity of instance-level annotations in UAV scenarios, we propose AeroTrack, a training-free unified framework. AeroTrack centers on periodic open-vocabulary detection, short-segment mask propagation, and cross-segment identity unification, reusing existing visual foundation models to enable UAV-OVVIS. Based on this framework, we instantiate five AeroTrack variants and construct AeroVIS, an evaluation benchmark for UAV-OVVIS containing 9 UAV object categories and 8,279 trajectories. Experiments show that AeroTrack substantially outperforms existing general video instance segmentation methods in UAV scenarios and demonstrates strong open-vocabulary robustness and generalization. To support future research, we release AeroTrack and AeroVIS as a unified framework and benchmark for UAV-OVVIS.

</details>


### [13] [LDFE: Laplacian Decoupled Feature Enhancement Block for Dual-Stream CNN-based RGB-IR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08076)
*Wenhao Dong,Xiaoyan Luo,Linlin Yang,Haodong Zhu,Xiaorong Shi,Guodong Guo,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拉普拉斯解耦特征增强模块（LDFE），用于双流CNN主干网络中RGB和红外图像的特征融合，通过全局-局部分解、去噪、融合和重建，利用全局状态空间增强模块（GS2E）和局部卷积相关增强模块（LC2E）分别处理全局和局部特征，在多个数据集上显著超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB和红外图像的互补信息可以显著增强极端条件下的目标检测性能。现有方法大多基于YOLO构建双流CNN主干进行特征提取，并聚焦于特征融合的设计，但在模态特性和结构特性的联合利用上仍有改进空间。

Method: 提出拉普拉斯解耦特征增强模块（LDFE），首先基于拉普拉斯金字塔将特征分解为全局和局部分量，然后分别通过GS2E（全局状态空间增强模块）和LC2E（局部卷积相关增强模块）进行去噪和融合。GS2E采用双分支架构，通过交叉模态注意力抑制主模态噪声，并利用状态空间模型捕获全局特征的长距离依赖；两个模态交替充当主/辅助角色以实现双向交互。LC2E抑制局部特征噪声，利用空间和通道维度结合三重卷积提取细粒度细节进行融合。

Result: 在M3FD、DroneVehicle、LLVIP、FLIR-Aligned、KAIST和VEDAI数据集上，mAP分别超越SOTA方法6.2%、3.7%、4.7%、2.3%、4.1%和2.0%。

Conclusion: LDFE的创新设计显著提升了RGB-IR目标检测性能，验证了全局-局部分解、双向交互和细粒度融合的有效性。

Abstract: The complementary information between RGB and IR images can significantly enhance object detection performance under extreme conditions. Existing methods prefer dual-stream CNN backbones built upon YOLO for feature extraction and focus on the design of feature fusion. In this paper, we introduce the Laplacian Decoupled Feature Enhancement block (LDFE) to fuse features from different stages of the dual-stream CNN backbone. By design, LDFE simultaneously considers the characteristics of modalities and structures for feature fusion by employing global-local decomposition, denoising, fusion, and reconstruction, sequentially. The LDFE first separates features into global and local components based on Laplacian Pyramid, and then performs denoising and fusion based on Global State Space Enhancement module (GS2E) and Local Convolutional Correlation Enhancement module (LC2E) separately. Specifically, the GS2E conducts a two-branch architecture for the main and auxiliary modalities. It dynamically suppresses noise in the main modality through cross-modal attention derived from the auxiliary modality, while employing a State Space Model to capture long-range dependencies within the global feature representations of the main modality. To obtain bidirectional interaction, the two modalities systematically alternate their main/auxiliary roles. Moreover, the LC2E suppresses noise in local features and leverages spatial and channel dimension along with triple convolution to extract fine-grained details for fusion. These innovative designs achieve a significant performance improvement, with mAP surpassing the SOTA methods 6.2%, 3.7%, 4.7%, 2.3%, 4.1% and 2.0% on M3FD, DroneVehicle, LLVIP, FLIR-Aligned, KAIST and VEDAI datasets,respectively.

</details>


### [14] [Mixture of Enhanced-View Experts for Multi-Query Vehicle ReID and A Large-Scale Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08085)
*Aihua Zheng,Jie Zhen,Chenglong Li,Jiaxiang Wang,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CAFNet的多查询车辆重识别方法，其核心是增强视图专家混合模块（EV-MoE），包含视图特征增强子模块（VFEM）和动态多视图融合子模块（DMFM），并引入多视图对齐损失（MAL）进行双向跨视图对比学习与重建约束。此外，还收集了大规模数据集LCRI-1K（1090个身份，107805张图像，23637个摄像头）。实验证明了CAFNet在多查询车辆ReID上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法特征融合过于简单，容易忽略重要视角信息及跨视角关系，导致多查询车辆重识别性能受限。

Method: 提出CAFNet框架，核心是增强视图专家混合模块（EV-MoE），由两部分组成：1) 视图特定特征增强子模块（VFEM）用于增强每个视图的特征表示；2) 动态多视图融合子模块（DMFM）通过MoE高效集成增强后的视图特征。同时引入多视图对齐损失（MAL），通过双向跨视图对比学习和重建约束来对齐多查询特征与单图像特征的一致性。还收集了大规模LCRI-1K数据集用于评估。

Result: 大量实验表明CAFNet在多查询车辆重识别问题中具有鲁棒性，在LCRI-1K等数据集上表现优异。

Conclusion: CAFNet通过增强视图专家混合和多视图对齐损失，有效解决了多查询车辆ReID中特征融合简单、忽视跨视图关系的问题，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-query vehicle ReID aims to leverage complementary information from diverse views for robust feature learning. However, current methods suffer from simplistic feature fusion and thus easily ignores some important view information and cross-view relationships. To handle these problems, this work presents a novel approach called Mixture of Enhanced-View Experts (EV-MoE), which enhances the feature representation of each view and efficiently integrate the view-specific enhanced features by MoE, for robust multi-query ReID. In particular, we design a mixture of enhanced-view experts module, which consists of two parts including view-specific feature enhancement sub-Module (VFEM) and dynamic multi-view fusion sub-Module (DMFM). Moreover, we further introduce Multi-view Alignment Loss (MAL), which aligns features through bidirectional crossview contrastive learning and reconstruction constraints, addressing the challenges of consistency between multi-query features and single-image features. In addition, to evaluate multi-query ReID in real-world environments, we collect LCRI-1K, a largescale vehicle ReID dataset with 1,090 identities, 107,805 images, across 23,637 cameras, where each vehicle appears in an average of 67.5 cameras, providing a comprehensive benchmark to test the robustness in complex environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate the robustness of CAFNet in addressing the multiquery vehicle ReID problem. The code is available at https: //github.com/xiaozhen28/CAFNet.

</details>


### [15] [GRE-Diff: Gaussian Room Embeddings for Structured Layout Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08086)
*Jing Wang,Haoran Xiong,Zihao Yan,Minglun Gong,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRE-Diff 是一个基于扩散模型的可控交互式框架，用于自动生成和编辑公寓楼层平面图。它通过高斯房间嵌入（GRE）将每个房间表示为连续的空间高斯分布，结合大语言模型解析的指令或图形用户界面交互，支持用户指定房间类型、数量、边界形状等约束，并生成高质量、可编辑的多边形布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计功能性和美学上连贯的楼层平面图需要探索巨大的房间排列空间，这对人类设计师而言极具挑战性。现有方法缺乏对用户约束的灵活支持和实时交互编辑能力，因此需要一种能够桥接AI自动化与人类创造力的可控生成框架。

Method: 提出 GRE-Diff 框架，核心是高斯房间嵌入（GRE），即用连续潜空间中的高斯分布建模每个房间的位置和范围。系统通过大语言模型解析自然语言指令或图形用户界面交互来获取用户约束（房间类型、数量、边界形状等），然后利用扩散模型生成多样且结构合理的平面图，并支持人机协同的实时编辑与迭代优化。

Result: 在 RPLAN 数据集上的大量实验表明，GRE-Diff 能够生成高质量、满足约束且可编辑的多边形布局，验证了该方法在可控性和交互性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: GRE-Diff 通过将AI驱动的自动化与人类创造力相结合，为空间设计提供了一种实用的解决方案，在楼层平面图生成领域迈出了重要一步，未来可进一步拓展到更复杂的建筑布局设计场景。

Abstract: Designing functional and aesthetically coherent floor plans requires exploring a vast space of possible room arrangements, a task that quickly becomes overwhelming for human designers. In this paper, we propose GRE-Diff, a controllable and interactive diffusion-based framework that automates the creation and editing of apartment floor plans under user-specified constraints.
  By combining AI-generated suggestions with real-time, human-in-the-loop editing, the system enables users to specify room types, room counts, boundary shapes, and editing operations through LLM-parsed instructions or GUI-based interaction.
  It then generates a diverse set of plausible and well-structured designs for refinement.
  At the core of our approach is Gaussian Room Embedding (GRE), a continuous latent representation that models each room as a spatial Gaussian distribution capturing its location and extent.
  Extensive experiments on the RPLAN dataset show that GRE-Diff produces high-quality, constraint-aware, and editable polygonal layouts, offering a practical step toward bridging AI-driven automation and human creativity in spatial design.

</details>


### [16] [EVIS: A Physics-Grounded Event Camera Plugin for NVIDIA Isaac Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08098)
*Linli Shi,Ruijun Zhang,Ziyun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EVIS是一个基于物理的事件相机插件，集成于NVIDIA Isaac Sim，可在物理仿真中生成高帧率、完全标注的事件流，支持异步像素参考更新、运动矢量插值加速、传感器噪声和运动模糊，弥合仿真与真实相机之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有微秒级时间分辨率、低延迟和高动态范围等优势，但针对特定机器人和场景的标注事件数据稀缺且采集成本高昂，制约了基于事件的感知与控制方法的发展。

Method: 实现了忠实于对数强度对比的事件模型，包含逐像素异步参考更新；从普通RGB相机迁移改动极小，可集成到任何Isaac Sim/Isaac Lab场景；提供插值选项，仅渲染稀疏关键帧，通过双向运动矢量扭曲合成中间帧，支持在单GPU上实时生成；可选传感器噪声和运动模糊模块。

Result: 生成的事件流可直接用于预训练事件网络的下游任务，实现实时生成，且通过噪声和运动模糊模块缩小了仿真与真实相机之间的差距。

Conclusion: EVIS为事件相机研究提供了一个高效、高质量的合成数据生成工具，有助于推动基于事件的感知与控制在实际机器人中的应用。

Abstract: Event cameras offer microsecond temporal resolution, low latency, and high dynamic range, making them attractive for robotics. However, labeled event-camera data for a specific robot and scene is scarce and expensive to collect, which slows the development of event-based perception and control. We present EVIS: a physics-grounded event camera plugin for NVIDIA Isaac Sim that generates high-rate, fully labeled event streams directly inside a physics simulator. The plugin implements a faithful log-intensity contrast event model with per-pixel asynchronous reference updates; it migrates from a normal RGB camera with few changes and integrates into any Isaac Sim / Isaac Lab scene, inheriting the simulator's physics and frame-perfect ground truth. It is fully configurable, and offers an interpolation option that renders only sparse keyframes and synthesizes the in-between frames through bidirectional motion-vector warping, making real-time generation on a single GPU possible. Optional sensor noise and motion blur further narrow the gap to real cameras. The generated streams are directly usable by pretrained event networks for downstream tasks. Code repository: https://github.com/spikelab-jhu/isaac-sim-event-camera-plugin

</details>


### [17] [VSRo-200: A Romanian Visual Speech Recognition Dataset for Studying Supervision and Multimodal Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08112)
*Iulia-Maria Udrea,Alexandra Diaconu,Bogdan Alexe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VSRo-200 是首个大规模罗马尼亚语视觉语音识别数据集，包含 200 小时的播客视频，其中 100 小时有人工标注，其余为伪标签。该工作系统研究了标注质量、领域泛化、多模态融合等问题，并在孤立词识别基准上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罗马尼亚语作为一种低资源语言，缺乏大规模的视觉语音识别数据集。现有数据集多集中于英语等高资源语言，无法充分研究低资源场景下的监督质量、领域泛化与多模态融合等问题。

Method: 构建了 VSRo-200 数据集：200 小时真实播客视频，其中 100 小时由人工转录，另外 100 小时由微调后的罗马尼亚语 ASR 模型生成伪标签；在此基础上建立了视觉语音识别基准，系统比较了人工标注与伪标签的性能差异，评估了领域偏移下的鲁棒性，并分析了噪声条件下的音视频融合效果。

Result: 人工标注在固定数据规模下表现更好，但伪标签可通过扩展数据量持续提升性能；多模态融合在噪声条件下显著优于纯音频模型；基于 VSRo-200 学到的表征可有效迁移到 LRRo 孤立词识别基准，大幅超越先前结果。

Conclusion: VSRo-200 为低资源视觉语音识别中的监督学习、领域泛化和多模态融合研究提供了一个新的测试平台，有望推动该方向的发展。

Abstract: We introduce VSRo-200, the first large-scale dataset for visual speech recognition (lip reading) in Romanian, comprising 200 hours of real-world podcast videos. All samples are annotated with pseudo-labels generated by a fine-tuned Romanian ASR model, while a subset of 100 hours is additionally transcribed by humans, enabling controlled analysis of supervision quality under a unified framework. Building on this dataset, we establish a benchmark for visual speech recognition in low-resource settings. We systematically study the impact of supervision quality, showing that while human annotations provide better performance at fixed data scales, pseudo-labels enable continued improvements through scalability. We further evaluate robustness under domain shift using curated out-of-distribution (OOD) test sets, and analyze audio-visual speech recognition (AVSR) under noisy conditions, where multimodal fusion significantly improves robustness compared to audio-only models. Finally, we demonstrate that representations learned on VSRo-200 transfer effectively to the LRRo benchmark for isolated word recognition, substantially outperforming previously reported results. Overall, VSRo-200 provides a new testbed for studying supervision, domain generalization, and multimodal fusion in low-resource visual speech recognition.

</details>


### [18] [Understanding and Mitigating the Video-Action Generalization Gap via Temporal Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08127)
*Utkarsh A. Mishra,Yongxin Chen,Danfei Xu,Yang Liu,Xi Chen,Jiayuan Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视频-动作模型（VAMs）在微调后丢失生成式视频基础模型组合先验的“视频-动作泛化差距”，引入时序比（TR）作为衡量动作头依赖未来潜在展开程度的注意力指标，并提出基于TR的自适应推理时引导方法，在LIBERO基准和真实任务上缓解了分布外与分布内组合泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频基础模型具有强大的组合先验，但世界-动作模型和视频-动作模型在机器人动作数据微调后往往丢失这些先验，存在视频-动作泛化差距，且标准设计选择无法产生可解释的模式，亟需系统性研究该差距的内在机制。

Method: 系统评估VAMs的设计空间，引入时序比（TR）——一种基于注意力的度量，衡量动作头对未来潜在展开相对于锚定当前帧的依赖程度；提出自适应推理时引导方法，根据TR动态放大组合视频条件信号。

Result: TR具有两个关键性质：模型对未来预测潜变量的结构依赖可预测组合泛化能力；TR根据任务阶段原生波动（规划时关注未来帧，操作时回归当前帧）。基于TR的自适应方法在LIBERO基准和真实任务上缓解了OOD-ID组合泛化差距。

Conclusion: TR是解释视频-动作模型中组合泛化差距的有效注意力度量，基于其原生波动模式的自适应引导方法能有效缓解组合泛化差距，为构建强组合泛化能力的视频-动作模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Generative video foundation models exhibit strong compositional priors, yet world-action models (WAMs) and video-action models (VAMs) often lose these priors after finetuning on robotic action data. We refer to this discrepancy as the video-action generalization gap. In this paper, we systematically investigate this gap by evaluating a comprehensive design space of VAMs, demonstrating that standard design choices yield no emergent explanation pattern. To explain this behavior, we introduce the Temporal Ratio (TR), an attention-based measure of how strongly the action head relies on future latent rollouts relative to the anchored current frame. TR has two key properties: first, a model's structural reliance on future-predictive latents, measured via TR, acts as a predictor of its compositional generalization capacity; second, it natively fluctuates based on task phase, shifting attention to future frames during planning and reverting to the present frame for precise manipulation. Finally, based on these findings, we propose an inference-time adaptive guidance method, which exploits this intrinsic feature attention pattern to dynamically amplify compositional video conditioning signals precisely when the policy relies on future rollouts. Evaluated on the LIBERO benchmark and real-world tasks, our approach mitigates the OOD-ID compositional generalization gap. More details: https://umishra.me/temporal-ratio/

</details>


### [19] [Unified Face Attack Detection via Fine-Grained Semantic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08156)
*Ning Jiang,Shijie Yu,Dingheng Zeng,Haiyang Yi,Yanhong Liu,Haifeng Shen,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于大规模MS-UFAD数据集的细粒度文本描述增强方法，并设计双对齐伪造网络(DAF-Net)利用文本信息进行人脸伪造检测，在视觉-only和粗粒度描述方法上均取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集缺乏伪造线索的详细文本描述，导致大多数方法将人脸攻击检测仅视为视觉识别任务，难以提取泛化性强且语义丰富的伪造表征。

Method: 在包含800万+攻击图像的大规模MS-UFAD数据集上，为每张图像补充细粒度的伪造线索文本描述；提出双对齐伪造网络(DAF-Net)以有效利用文本信息。

Result: 该方法提取出更具泛化性和语义意义的伪造表征，在视觉-only方法和基于粗粒度描述的方法上均取得更优性能。

Conclusion: 引入细粒度文本描述并设计文本-视觉对齐网络，能有效提升人脸伪造检测的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: The growing applications of facial recognition systems are accompanied by increasingly diverse security threats. Existing datasets lack detailed textual descriptions of forgery cues, leading most prior methods to treat face attack detection primarily as a visual recognition task. In this paper, building upon the large-scale MS-UFAD dataset which contains over 8 million attack images, we enrich each image with a fine-grained textual description of forgery cues. Furthermore, we propose a Dual Alignment Forgery Network(DAF-Net) to better leverage these textual information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach extracts more generalizable and semantically meaningful forgery representations from attack images, outperforming both vision-only methods and approaches based on coarse-grained descriptions.

</details>


### [20] [ProsMAE: Multi-Source MAE Pretraining for ISUP Grade Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08162)
*Anna Jung,Kyeonghun Kim,Youngung Han,Eunseob Choi,Jiwon Yang,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProsMAE is a multi-source Masked Autoencoder pretraining framework for histopathology representation learning using tiles from PANDA, CAMELYON17, and BRACS datasets. It achieves better ISUP grade classification (higher QWK) than vanilla MAE frozen linear-probe baseline on PANDA split, but further split robustness evaluation is needed.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whole slide images are challenging due to gigapixel scale, stain variation, scanner differences, tissue artifacts, and limited expert annotations, motivating the need for robust representation learning that can generalize across diverse data sources.

Method: ProsMAE uses a multi-source Masked Autoencoder pretrained on tiles from PANDA, CAMELYON17, and BRACS datasets to expose the encoder to diverse tissue morphology and acquisition conditions. The learned encoder is then transferred for ISUP grade classification via ProsCLS with a frozen encoder and linear classification head.

Result: ProsMAE achieved a higher mean validation quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) than the vanilla MAE frozen linear-probe baseline on the evaluated disjoint PANDA split.

Conclusion: The multi-source MAE pretraining improves histopathology representation learning for ISUP grading, but repeated-split evaluation is necessary to further establish robustness across different split compositions.

Abstract: Whole slide images (WSIs) provide rich diagnostic information for computational pathology, but their gigapixel scale, stain variation, scanner differences, tissue artifacts, and limited expert annotation make robust model training challenging. This paper presents a multi-source Masked Autoencoder (MAE) framework, named ProsMAE, for histopathology representation learning. Tiles from Prostate cANcer graDe Assessment (PANDA), CAncer MEtastases in LYmph nOdes challeNge 2017 (CAMELYON17), and BReAst Carcinoma Subtyping (BRACS) are used for ProsMAE pretraining to expose the encoder to diverse tissue morphology and acquisition conditions. The learned encoder is transferred for International Society of Urological Pathology (ISUP) grade classification through ProsCLS, using a frozen encoder and a linear classification head. ProsMAE achieved a higher mean validation quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) than the vanilla MAE frozen linear-probe baseline under the evaluated disjoint PANDA split. Repeated-split evaluation remains necessary to further establish robustness across split compositions.

</details>


### [21] [Continual Test-Time Adaptation in Computer Vision: Methods, Benchmarks, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08164)
*Sarthak Kumar Maharana,Shambhavi Mishra,Yunbei Zhang,Shuaicheng Niu,Taki Hasan Rafi,Jihun Hamm,Marco Pedersoli,Jose Dolz,Yunhui Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对持续测试时适应（CTTA）进行了全面综述，形式化定义了问题，分析了多样化的持续域偏移模式，提出了包含优化策略、参数高效方法和架构方法的三层分类体系，系统回顾了代表性方法并提供了比较基准与实验结果，最后讨论了当前局限与未来方向（如基础模型和黑盒系统适应）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在训练和测试数据同分布时表现优异，但实际部署中数据分布持续变化导致性能下降。CTTA旨在解决无源数据、无标签目标域条件下的在线适应问题，同时缓解灾难性遗忘和噪声伪标签导致的误差累积两个关键失效模式。

Method: 本文提出层次化分类法，将现有CTTA方法分为三类：(1) 优化策略（熵最小化、伪标签、参数恢复）；(2) 参数高效方法（归一化层适配、自适应参数选择）；(3) 架构方法（教师-学生框架、适配器、视觉提示、掩码建模）。系统综述代表性方法并在标准评估设置下进行对比基准测试。

Result: 提供了CTTA问题的形式化定义、多样化的持续域偏移模式分析、系统的分类法以及标准化评估下的比较基准和实验结果，揭示了不同方法的优势与局限。

Conclusion: CTTA虽取得进展，但仍面临挑战，未来方向包括基础模型的持续适应和黑盒系统的适应。本文为鲁棒的持续测试时适应研究提供了路线图。

Abstract: Deep neural nets achieve remarkable performance when training and test data share the same distribution, but this assumption frequently breaks in real-world deployment, where data undergoes continual distributional shifts. Continual Test-Time Adaptation (CTTA) addresses this challenge by adapting pretrained models to non-stationary target distributions on-the-fly, without access to source data or labeled targets, while mitigating two critical failure modes: catastrophic forgetting of source knowledge and error accumulation from noisy pseudo-labels over extended time horizons. In this comprehensive survey, we formally define the CTTA problem, analyze the diverse continual domain shift patterns that characterize different evaluation protocols, and propose a hierarchical taxonomy that categorizes existing methods into three families: optimization-based strategies (entropy minimization, pseudo-labeling, parameter restoration), parameter-efficient methods (normalization layer adaptation, adaptive parameter selection), and architecture-based approaches (teacher-student frameworks, adapters, visual prompting, masked modeling). We systematically review representative methods within each category and present comparative benchmarks and experimental results across standard evaluation settings. Finally, we discuss limitations of current approaches and highlight emerging research directions, including adaptation of foundation models and black-box systems, providing a roadmap for future research in robust continual test-time adaptation. We encourage visiting our repository at [https://github.com/sarthaxxxxx/Awesome-Continual-Test-Time-Adaptation](https://github.com/sarthaxxxxx/Awesome-Continual-Test-Time-Adaptation)

</details>


### [22] [Attention-Based Segmentation of WMHs and Differentiation of Vascular vs. Demyelinating Lesions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08171)
*Aina Tur-Serrano,Gabriel Moyà-Alcover,Francisco J. Perales López*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结合注意力机制的分割（如BAM、CBAM、Attention U-Net）与特征驱动分类的方法，用于区分血管性和脱髓鞘性白质高信号（WMHs），在多个公开数据集上验证了该方法的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WMHs在FLAIR图像上表现相似，但病因不同（血管性与脱髓鞘性），临床鉴别诊断困难，需要更准确高效的分类方法。

Method: 采用注意力机制（BAM、CBAM）和Attention U-Net架构进行WMH分割，结合patch-based学习和2.5D策略提升检测效果；分割后提取病变形态学特征，用于分类血管性和脱髓鞘性WMHs。

Result: 在5个公开数据集上验证，注意力分割与特征分类结合的方法在区分血管性和脱髓鞘性白质病变方面表现良好，但需在更大临床队列中进一步验证。

Conclusion: 注意力机制的分割与特征驱动的分类相结合是区分血管性和脱髓鞘性WMHs的有前景方向，但仍需更大样本的临床验证。

Abstract: White Matter Hyperintensities (WMHs) are commonly observed in brain Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) scans. They are associated with various neurological conditions, including vascular and inflammatory demyelinating diseases. Despite differing in etiology, WMHs from these conditions often appear similar on Fluid Attenuated Inversion Recovery (FLAIR) images. This similarity makes differential diagnosis challenging. In this work, we highlight the potential of combining attention-based segmentation with feature-driven classification. This approach supports more accurate and efficient classification between vascular and demyelinating white matter pathologies. For segmentation, we evaluate the effectiveness of attention mechanisms, specifically the Bottleneck Attention Module (BAM) and the Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM). We also test different architectures, particularly Attention U-Net. In addition, we explore advanced training strategies, such as patch-based learning and a 2.5D approach, to enhance lesion detection. After segmentation, we extract morphological features from the lesion masks. We then use them to classify WMHs based on their underlying cause. Our experiments utilize five publicly available datasets with diverse imaging protocols to promote model generalizability, despite limited sample sizes. The results suggest that attention-based segmentation and feature-driven classification offer a promising direction for discriminating vascular and demyelinating white matter lesions. Further validation in larger clinical cohorts is still needed.

</details>


### [23] [LEEVLA: Seeing What Matters in Latent Environment Evolution for Vision-Language-Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08182)
*Qi Lyu,Baicheng Liu,Xudong Wang,Jiahua Dong,Lianqing Liu,Zhi Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LEEVLA是一种视觉-语言-动作（VLA）架构，通过漂移引导动态优先级（DGDP）和结构化特征流生成（SFFG），实现任务关键证据的显式引导与隐式潜在世界结构演化的结合，在VLA基准测试中持续优于以往方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将所有视觉token统一处理并用人工选择的因素进行推理，缺乏强调任务关键证据的机制且忽略潜在因素，难以覆盖复杂动态场景。

Method: 提出LEEVLA架构，包含两个核心组件：(1) 漂移引导动态优先级（DGDP），由动态位置优先级（DPP）和语义漂移引导（SDG）组成，用于识别显著且与指令相关的区域；(2) 结构化特征流生成（SFFG），通过原型到外围（P2P）预测建模优先级特征的潜在演化，并采用互邻对比（MC）损失保持邻域拓扑一致性，形成"哪里-如何"的训练框架。

Result: 在VLA基准上的大量实验表明，LEEVLA持续优于先前方法，验证了显式任务证据引导和结构化潜在推理对可扩展VLA的重要性。

Conclusion: 显式任务证据引导和结构化潜在推理对于实现可扩展的VLA模型至关重要，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models aim to map multimodal inputs to robot actions. However, most existing approaches struggle to cover complex dynamic scenarios due to treating all visual tokens uniformly and reasoning with human-selected factors, which lack mechanisms to emphasize task-critical evidence and ignore underlying factors. To address this issue, we propose LEEVLA, a VLA architecture for seeing what matters in Latent Environment Evolution that explicitly guides the model toward informative regions while preserving the structured evolution of latent world representations. To identify salient and instruction-relevant regions, we introduce drift-guided dynamic prioritization (DGDP), which combines dynamic position prioritization (DPP) with semantic drift guidance (SDG) to guide the VLA agent where to attend during training. On top of this, we introduce structured feature flow generation (SFFG), which models how these prioritized features should evolve in latent space via prototype-to-periphery (P2P) prediction, and a mutual-neighborhood contrastive (MC) loss to maintain topological consistency among neighborhoods. Together, DGDP and SFFG form a task-aware "where-how" training framework. Extensive experiments on VLA benchmarks show that LEEVLA consistently outperforms prior methods, confirming that explicit task-evidence guidance and structured latent reasoning are both crucial for scalable VLA. Our code is available at https://github.com/LyuQi127/LEEVLA.

</details>


### [24] [Leveraging Color Naming for Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08185)
*David Serrano-Lozano,Luis Herranz,Michael S. Brown,Javier Vazquez-Corral*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NamedCurves+ 是一种基于颜色命名概念的图像增强方法，通过为每种命名颜色学习独立的色调曲线，并结合Transformer捕捉空间依赖，实现了可解释、可交互且性能优于现有技术的图像润饰、色调映射和曝光校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法虽能复制专家编辑风格，但存在两大问题：(1) 缺乏可解释性，(2) 参数化方式不便于用户调整。这促使作者探索一种既保持高性能又具备可解释性和用户交互能力的图像增强方法。

Method: NamedCurves+ 方法：(1) 借鉴颜色命名概念（一组通用的熟悉颜色），将颜色名称集成到学习框架中；(2) 为每种命名颜色通过色调曲线进行全局调整；(3) 引入Transformer模块捕捉空间依赖，实现上下文感知的局部编辑；(4) 支持用户交互，允许灵活修改单个色调曲线以根据个人偏好精修图像。

Result: 在图像润饰、色调映射和曝光校正等任务上的大量实验表明，NamedCurves+ 的性能优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: NamedCurves+ 兼具可解释性（色调曲线明确表示每种颜色名称对增强的贡献）和交互性（用户可自定义润饰过程获得个性化结果），在多个图像增强任务上达到了最优性能。

Abstract: Enhancing images to make them visually appealing is a persistent challenge in computer vision. Many deep-learning methods train models on paired datasets to replicate expert editing styles. However, these approaches struggle with two key issues: (1) interpretability and (2) a parametrization suitable for user adjustments. To address these challenges, we present NamedCurves+, an approach inspired by the concept of Color Naming, a universal set of familiar colors widely used in software tools for intuitive editing. Our method integrates color names into a learning-based framework, enabling global adjustments for each named color through tone curves. To address local image variations, we incorporate a transformer block that captures spatial dependencies, enabling context-aware edits across the image. NamedCurves+ enhances the retouching process's interpretability and supports user interaction, allowing flexible modifications of individual tone curves to refine the retouched image according to personal preferences. Extensive experiments on tasks such as image retouching, tone mapping, and exposure correction demonstrate that NamedCurves+ outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Notably, our approach is both explainable, as the tone curves explicitly represent how each color name contributes to the enhancement, and interactive, allowing users to customize the retouching process and achieve results tailored to their liking.

</details>


### [25] [Dual-Correlation Hypergraph Network for Unaligned RGBT Video Object Detection and A Large-scale Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08191)
*Qishun Wang,Yapeng Li,Bin Luo,Zhengzheng Tu,Chenglong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了双相关超图网络（DHNet），通过显式建模时序相关性和跨模态空间相关性来解决RGB-T热成像视频目标检测中的空间错位问题，并构建了大规模、场景多样的基准数据集DVT-VOD1000。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-T热成像视频目标检测在恶劣条件下优于纯RGB检测，但RGB-T图像对之间存在空间错位问题，且缺乏大规模、场景多样的基准数据集用于全面评估。

Method: 提出DHNet，包含两个核心模块：1）基于补丁的空间对齐模块（PSAM），在局部区域级别顺序对齐多模态特征；2）双超图融合模块（DHFM），分别构建时序超图和多模态超图以通过双相关学习增强目标判别能力。同时构建了包含1000个视频序列、103,464个RGB-T图像对的大规模数据集DVT-VOD1000。

Result: 在VT-VOD50和自建的DVT-VOD1000两个数据集上，DHNet均取得了最先进的检测精度。

Conclusion: DHNet通过双相关超图网络有效解决了RGB-T视频目标检测中的空间错位问题，并且大规模数据集DVT-VOD1000为领域研究提供了新的基准。代码和数据集将公开。

Abstract: RGB-Thermal (RGBT) Video Object Detection (VOD) has gained significant traction due to its ability to overcome the limitations of conventional RGB-based VOD under challenging conditions. However, spatial misalignment commonly exists between RGBT image pairs. To address this, we propose a Dual-Correlation Hypergraph Network (DHNet) that captures high-dimensional complementary information by explicitly modeling two types of correlations: temporal correlation across consecutive frames and spatial correlation from cross-modal features. Specifically, we first design a Patch-based Spatial Alignment Module (PSAM) to sequentially align the multimodal features at the local region level. Subsequently, we introduce a Dual Hypergraph Fusion Module (DHFM), which constructs separate temporal and multimodal hypergraphs to enhance object discriminability through dual-correlation learning. Furthermore, the field currently lacks a large-scale, scene-diverse benchmark dataset for comprehensive evaluation. To address this gap, we construct DVT-VOD1000, a large-scale RGBT VOD dataset containing 1,000 video sequences with 103,464 RGBT image pairs. The dataset covers diverse scenarios, including campuses, parks, transportation, rural areas, night scenes, rain, and snow. Comprehensive experiments on VT-VOD50 and our DVT-VOD1000 demonstrate that DHNet achieves state-of-the-art detection accuracy. The dataset and source code will be made publicly available on https://github.com/tzz-ahu/ to support academic research.

</details>


### [26] [Dive Into the Implicit Biases of Low-rank Vision-language Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08194)
*Mingjia Shi,Shuo Wang,Xiaobo Wang,Sifan Zhou,Kai Wang,Tianyu Fu,Chenxu Zhao,Anyang Su,Ping Jiang,Minghui Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文挑战了视觉-语言对齐阶段必须进行全参数更新的传统观点，提出在LLM上应用低秩适配（LoRA）进行对齐。实验表明，低秩对齐不仅降低了计算成本，还在大多数基准上超越了全参数对齐。作者系统表征了低秩对齐的隐式偏差：从幻觉性转向保守性，保留了全参数对齐破坏的逐token线性可分性（LS-curse），并维持了更均匀、结构稳定的视觉表征。理论上证明了低秩对齐倾向于平坦梯度的参数子空间和抗扰动的特征子空间，解释了其结构保持行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视觉-语言对齐需要全参数更新，计算成本高。作者质疑这一做法，探究在对齐阶段对LLM使用低秩适配（LoRA）的效果，旨在降低计算开销并可能提升性能。

Method: 在视觉-语言对齐阶段，对大型语言模型（LLM）应用低秩适配（LoRA），而非全参数微调。通过实验对比低秩对齐与全参数对齐的性能，并系统分析低秩对齐的隐式偏差，包括幻觉性、逐token线性可分性（LS-curse）以及视觉表征的几何性质。理论上建立了两个定理，分别关于参数子空间的平坦梯度偏好和特征子空间的鲁棒性偏好。

Result: 低秩对齐在大多数基准上优于全参数对齐，同时降低计算成本。低秩对齐使模型行为从幻觉性转向保守性，保留了全参数对齐破坏的逐token线性可分性（LS-curse），并产生更均匀、结构稳定的视觉表征。理论证明低秩对齐偏好平坦梯度的参数子空间和抗扰动的特征子空间。

Conclusion: 低秩适配可以有效地替代全参数更新进行视觉-语言对齐，不仅更高效而且性能更好。低秩对齐的隐式偏差有助于保持模态特定知识，避免过早融合实体级语义，为理解低秩适配在对齐阶段的作用提供了原则性解释。

Abstract: Vision-language alignment, the stage that bridges pretrained vision encoders and large language models, is widely treated as a form of pretraining requiring full-parameter updates. We challenge this view and investigate what happens when low-rank adaptation is applied to the LLM during this stage instead. We find that low-rank alignment not only reduces computational costs but also outperforms full-parameter alignment on most benchmarks. To understand this phenomenon, we systematically characterize the implicit biases introduced by low-rank adaptation during alignment. Empirically, we find that low-rank alignment shifts model behavior from hallucinatory to conservative and preserves per-token linear separability of visual features that full-parameter alignment disrupts, a phenomenon we term LS-curse. Geometrically, low rank aligned models exhibit more homogeneous and structurally stable visual representations, maintaining modality-specific knowledge rather than prematurely fusing entity-level semantics. Theoretically, we establish two theorems showing that low-rank alignment induces preferences for parameter subspaces with flat gradients and feature subspaces robust to perturbations, providing a principled explanation for the observed structure-preserving behavior. Extensive experiments cover ablation over 100 alignment configurations, three families of low-rank operators, and various rank, encoder, and other settings.

</details>


### [27] [Unpaired Joint Distribution Modeling via Multi-Scale Image Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08198)
*Yihang Zou,Hui Zhang,Zuowei Shen,Chenglong Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LUD-MSR框架，一种基于潜变量模型的概率方法，用于从边缘观测中学习联合分布。该框架通过多尺度图像表示（MSR）在域一致性与信息保留之间取得更优平衡，并在冷冻电镜（cryo-EM）去噪等真实基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习联合分布时仅能获取边缘观测数据，由于可行耦合的歧义性，该问题具有固有的不适定性。现有方法难以同时保证域一致性并保留关键信息，因此需要一种能够有效建模联合分布并平衡上述矛盾的框架。

Method: 提出LUD-MSR潜变量概率框架，通过引入辅助表示来建模联合分布，并仅利用边缘数据优化证据下界（ELBO）。进一步设计多尺度图像表示（MSR）映射，在粗尺度上利用结构相似性同时抑制域特定变化，以平衡表示学习中域一致性与信息保留之间的权衡。

Result: 在温和假设下建立了分布近似误差的上界，揭示了域一致性与信息保留之间的权衡关系。与现有方法相比，MSR实现了更优的平衡。在真实世界去噪基准（包括冷冻电镜图像）上的实验证明了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: LUD-MSR框架通过多尺度表示有效解决了从边缘观测学习联合分布的不适定问题，在域一致性和信息保留之间取得了更好的折中，适用于实际去噪任务（如cryo-EM）。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of learning a joint distribution from marginal observations, which is inherently ill-posed due to the ambiguity of feasible couplings. We propose LUD-MSR, a latent-variable probabilistic framework that models the joint distribution via auxiliary representations and optimizes evidence lower bounds using only marginal data. Under mild assumptions, we establish an upper bound on the distribution approximation error. This analysis reveals a trade-off in representation learning between domain consistency and information preservation. To address this trade-off, we introduce a Multi-Scale image Representation (MSR) mapping that exploits structural similarity at coarse scales while suppressing domain-specific variations. We show that MSR achieves a more favorable balance of this trade-off compared to existing approaches. Experiments on real-world denoising benchmarks, including cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM), demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [28] [TMI: Text-to-Image Meets Image-to-Image for Complementary Data Synthesis to Boost Long-Tailed Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08201)
*Hyeonseop Song,Seokhun Choi,Hoseok Do*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混合T2I生成与上下文感知I2I编辑的实例分割数据增强方法，结合教师-学生方案过滤噪声标签，并设计VRAIN编辑器针对性增强稀有类，在LVIS上实现AP提升+4.0，稀有类AP提升+9.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模词汇实例分割受困于长尾类别分布和细粒度类间模糊性，现有数据合成方法（T2I和复制粘贴）各有局限：T2I继承噪声伪标签且对稀有类表现差，复制粘贴损害上下文真实性。

Method: 提出混合管道：(1) T2I分支生成广泛场景多样性，配合教师-学生方案仅保留提示指定类别的可靠标签；(2) VRAIN (上下文感知I2I编辑器) 将高置信度实例插入语义合理的野外场景位置，实现语义连贯且视觉自然的编辑，减少域差距并实现针对性增强。

Result: 在LVIS基准上，总体AP提升最多+4.0点，稀有类AP提升最多+9.5点，且随骨干网络容量扩展效果显著提升。

Conclusion: 混合T2I与上下文感知I2I编辑的范式能有效解决长尾分布和类间模糊性，VRAIN编辑器对稀有类增强尤为有效，方法具有可扩展性。

Abstract: Large-vocabulary instance segmentation is constrained by long-tailed category distributions and fine-grained inter-class ambiguity. While data synthesis offers a promising alternative, current paradigms have complementary limitations: text-to-image (T2I) methods inherit noisy pseudo-labels and struggle on rare classes, whereas copy-paste methods compromise contextual realism. To address these issues, we propose a hybrid pipeline coupling T2I generation with context-aware image-to-image (I2I) editing. The T2I branch provides broad category and scene diversity, while a teacher-student scheme ensures label reliability by selectively retaining only prompt-specified categories. To strengthen supervision for rare classes, we introduce VRAIN (Verified Rare-class Augmentation via INstructed editing), a novel I2I editor. VRAIN inserts high-confidence instances at semantically appropriate locations within in-the-wild scenes, yielding semantically coherent and visually natural edits that reduce domain gaps and enable targeted augmentation. On the LVIS benchmark, our method surpasses existing baselines, improving overall AP by up to +4.0 points and rare-class AP by up to +9.5 points, while scaling effectively with backbone capacity. Our project page is available at https://seokhunchoi.github.io/TMI

</details>


### [29] [Metrics or Mirage? An Audit of Evaluation Inconsistencies in Colonoscopy Polyp Segmentation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08203)
*Aisha Urooj,Zain Ul Abdien,Neelu Madan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了2015-2026年间27篇结肠镜息肉分割论文，发现三个结构性问题：忽略Hausdorff距离（边界精度指标）、存在至少5种不兼容的训练/测试划分协议导致Dice分数不可比、缺乏统计显著性检验。重新评估五个代表性模型后，证实报告指标掩盖了边界和召回失败，“最佳”模型随指标变化，排名在随机划分中反转。提出了五点评分报告清单（PSRC）作为领域适配的轻量级修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前结肠镜息肉分割领域的进展主要通过少量公共基准上的排行榜比较来报告，但这种进展难以验证。作者系统审计发现社区在模型评估方式上存在三个结构性问题，表明通用度量指南尚未有效渗透到该子领域，亟需领域适配的评估改进。

Method: 系统审计2015-2026年间27篇结肠镜息肉分割论文的评估实践，识别三个结构性问题；重新评估五个代表性模型，在三种受控协议下使用统一评分器，分析指标掩盖的问题。

Result: 27篇论文中25篇省略Hausdorff距离；至少5种不兼容的协议共存于Kvasir-SEG和CVC-ClinicDB两个数据集；26篇论文缺乏统计显著性检验。重新评估发现报告指标掩盖了边界和召回失败，“最佳”模型随选择的指标而变化，排名在随机划分中会反转。

Conclusion: 提出五点评分报告清单（PSRC）作为轻量级、领域适配的改进方案，以解决当前评估中的结构性问题，提高报告的可比较性和可靠性。

Abstract: Progress in colonoscopy polyp segmentation is routinely reported through leaderboard comparisons on a small set of public benchmarks. We argue that this apparent progress is difficult to verify: a systematic audit of \textbf{27 papers} published between 2015 and 2026 reveals three structural problems in how the community evaluates models. \textbf{First}, 25 of 27 papers \textit{omit the Hausdorff distance}. Hausdorff distance is a boundary-accuracy metric with direct clinical relevance for detecting flat or small polyps, and is a standard in radiotherapy segmentation. \textbf{Second}, at least five \textit{incompatible train/test split protocols} co-exist across papers reporting results on the same two datasets (Kvasir-SEG and CVC-ClinicDB), making published Dice scores non-comparable even when they appear in the same leaderboard column. \textbf{Third}, 26 of 27 papers make \textit{performance claims without any statistical significance test}. Strikingly, four papers published \emph{after} the Metrics Reloaded framework~\cite{metricsreloaded2024} (Maier-Hein et al., \textit{Nature Methods} 2024) perpetuate these same problems, suggesting that general-purpose metric guidance has not yet reached the colonoscopy sub-community. To show these problems are not merely cosmetic, we re-evaluate five representative models under three controlled protocols with a single uniform scorer, and find that the reported metric conceals large boundary and recall failures, that the ``best'' model changes with the metric, and that near-tied rankings reverse across random splits. We propose a five-point \textbf{Polyp Segmentation Reporting Checklist}~(PSRC) as a lightweight, domain-adapted corrective.

</details>


### [30] [Benchmark Evaluation of Feredated Learning on Multi-organ Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08219)
*Junbin Mao,Xu Tian,Jianchun Zhu,Ludi Li,Jin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MobenFL，一个综合性的联邦医学影像基准，整合了20种前沿联邦学习算法和22个医学影像数据集，覆盖12个关键器官，并系统评估性能、效率、隐私保护及真实临床场景，为联邦学习在医学领域的可靠临床应用提供统一评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗数据的隐私需求及其跨器官、模态的巨大差异阻碍了医学AI的临床落地。联邦学习虽有望解决这些问题，但现有基准未能充分纳入最新算法，局限于单一器官或模态，且过度强调模型准确性，难以全面评估联邦学习在真实医疗环境中的整体效用。

Method: 构建MobenFL基准，集成20种前沿FL算法和22个医学影像数据集，覆盖人体12个关键器官；评估维度不仅包括性能，还系统纳入算法效率、隐私保护能力等关键指标，并针对不同疾病、设备和成像模态的真实临床场景进行专项评估。

Result: MobenFL在广度上超越了现有基准，提供了涵盖多器官、多模态、多评估维度的全面评估框架，为联邦学习在医学领域的临床应用提供了深入的综合评价。

Conclusion: MobenFL作为统一的评估标准，能够推动联邦学习技术向可靠的临床应用迈进，填补了现有基准在算法覆盖、数据多样性和评估维度上的不足。

Abstract: The privacy requirements of medical data and its substantial variations across organs and modalities hinder the clinical implementation of medical AI. Federated learning (FL) is a feasible approach to overcome these challenges. Due to the continuous emergence of FL algorithms and the highly heterogeneous nature of medical data, objectively evaluating their performance in real-world clinical settings remains difficult. Therefore, a comprehensive federated medical imaging benchmark, serving as a unified evaluation standard, is crucial for advancing the technology toward reliable clinical application. Existing federated medical imaging benchmarks have not yet adequately incorporated state-of-the-art algorithms, are limited to data from single organs or modalities, and overly emphasize model accuracy, making it difficult to comprehensively assess the overall efficacy of FL in real-world medical environments. To address these challenges, we developed the MobenFL benchmark. This benchmark integrates 20 cutting-edge FL algorithms and 22 medical imaging datasets, covering 12 critical organs across the human body, surpassing existing benchmark in breadth. In terms of evaluation dimensions, MobenFL not only assesses performance but also systematically incorporates key metrics such as algorithmic efficiency and privacy protection capabilities. Additionally, it conducts specialized evaluations for complex real-world clinical scenarios involving different diseases, devices, and imaging modalities, thereby providing a comprehensive and in-depth evaluation framework for the clinical application of FL in the medical field.

</details>


### [31] [LUMI: Tokenizer-Agnostic LLM-Based Lossless Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08221)
*Chris Xing Tian,Chengkai Wu,Ziyu Wang,Rongqun Lin,Kecheng Chen,Xiandong Meng,Haoliang Li,Shiqi Wang,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LUMI is a tokenizer-agnostic framework for lossless RGB image compression using frozen LLM backbones, replacing pixel-as-text tokenization with a pixel embedding module and achieving competitive compression rates across diverse image domains.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM-based lossless image compression methods couple compression to tokenizer behavior, vocabulary-specific numeric tokens, and model-family-specific adaptation, limiting generality and cross-domain robustness.

Method: LUMI replaces pixel-as-text tokenization with a pixel embedding module that maps raw intensity and channel information into the LLM's continuous embedding space, introduces intra-patch position encoding to retain 2D spatial structure, and uses a 256-way prediction head for pixel-level probabilities. Only pixel embedding, position encoding, soft-prefix parameters, and prediction head are trained; LLM backbone remains frozen.

Result: Experiments on natural, medical, and remote-sensing images with LLaMA, Qwen, and Gemma backbones show LUMI provides a unified interface across tokenizer families, achieves competitive compression rates, and improves cross-domain robustness over tokenizer-based LLM compression baselines.

Conclusion: LLM-based lossless image compression is better formulated as pixel-space adaptation of frozen foundation models rather than tokenizer-specific language-symbol modeling.

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based lossless image compression methods typically represent pixel data through the native text interface of a pretrained model, converting pixel values into token sequences that the LLM processes through its vocabulary head. This design shows that pretrained language models can provide probability estimates for image coding, but it also couples compression to tokenizer behavior, vocabulary-specific numeric tokens, and model-family-specific adaptation. In this paper, we present LUMI (LLM-based Unified Model-agnostic lossless Image compression), a tokenizer-agnostic framework for lossless RGB image compression with frozen LLM backbones. LUMI replaces pixel-as-text tokenization with a pixel embedding module that maps raw intensity and channel information into the continuous embedding space of the LLM. It further introduces intra-patch position encoding to retain two-dimensional spatial structure after flattening, and uses a 256-way prediction head to produce probabilities over the native pixel alphabet. Only the pixel embedding, position encoding, soft-prefix parameters, and prediction head are trained, while the LLM backbone remains fixed. Experiments on natural, medical, and remote-sensing image benchmarks with LLaMA, Qwen, and Gemma backbones show that LUMI provides a unified interface across tokenizer families, achieves competitive compression rates, and improves cross-domain robustness over tokenizer-based LLM compression baselines. These results formulate LLM-based lossless image compression as pixel-space adaptation of frozen foundation models rather than tokenizer-specific language-symbol modeling.

</details>


### [32] [Multimodal 3D LUT Generation via StatLUT with Statistical Features for Photorealistic Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08227)
*Yifan Wang,Zhixiang Hao,Yu Wang,Congchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StatLUT 是一个面向多模态真实感风格迁移的 3D LUT 生成框架，通过 Lab 统计特征提取器解耦颜色与结构、用 Transformer Seq2Seq 预测拓扑平滑的 3D LUT、以及用轻量 Diffusion Transformer 实现文本驱动色彩调控，在标准基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PST 方法存在三大瓶颈：①基于预训练编码器的语义纠缠导致非自然空间扭曲；②像素级映射忽略色域拓扑引发色彩带状伪影；③缺乏直观的文本驱动多模态控制能力。为突破这些限制，需要一种解耦颜色-结构、保持色域拓扑、支持文本引导的新范式。

Method: 提出 StatLUT 多模态框架：① Lab-Extractor，避开传统编码器，直接从 Lab 颜色空间提取空间无关的统计特征（均值/方差等），从根本上解耦颜色分布与结构语义；② MR-Mapper，将 LUT 生成建模为基于 Transformer 的 Seq2Seq 翻译任务，预测拓扑平滑的 3D LUT，消除颜色带状伪影；③ H-Diffuser，一个轻量级 Diffusion Transformer，能够从自然语言提示直接合成统计特征，实现灵活文本驱动的色彩分级。

Result: 在标准基准上的大量实验表明，StatLUT 在视觉质量和量化指标上均显著优于当前最先进方法，实现了高鲁棒且灵活的多模态真实感风格迁移。

Conclusion: StatLUT 通过统计特征解耦、拓扑平滑 LUT 生成和文本驱动扩散模型三个创新设计，系统性解决了 PST 中的语义纠缠、色域拓扑缺失和单模态限制问题，为真实感风格迁移提供了更稳健、更灵活的多模态范式。

Abstract: Photorealistic Style Transfer (PST) aims to transfer the color and tonal style of a reference to a content image while strictly preserving its structural integrity. However, existing deep learning-based methods inherently suffer from semantic entanglement caused by pre-trained image encoders, leading to unnatural spatial distortions. Moreover, current pixel-level mapping paradigms often ignore color gamut topology, resulting in color banding, while also lacking the multimodal capability for intuitive text-driven control. To address these bottlenecks, we propose StatLUT, an innovative multimodal framework for 3D LUT generation. First, we bypass traditional encoders and introduce a Lab-Extractor to derive spatially-agnostic statistical features, fundamentally decoupling color distributions from structural semantics to ensure artifact-free rendering. Second, we formulate LUT generation as a Transformer-based Seq2Seq translation task, utilizing a Multi-dimensional Residual Mapper (MR-Mapper) to predict topologically smooth 3D LUTs. Finally, to break the single-modal barrier, we propose the H-Diffuser, a lightweight Diffusion Transformer that directly synthesizes statistical features from natural language prompts, enabling flexible text-driven color grading. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that StatLUT significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both visual quality and quantitative metrics, pioneering a highly robust and flexible paradigm for multimodal photorealistic style transfer.

</details>


### [33] [TVTA: Trajectory-Aware Viseme-Guided Temporal Aggregation for Event-Based Lip Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08236)
*Jingrong Zheng,Hongwei Ren,Xiangqian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有事件驱动唇读方法在时间建模上的不足（空间压缩过早、弱约束问题），提出包含轨迹感知差分聚合（TDA）、视素引导聚合（VGA）和EMA教师-学生训练策略的增强框架，在DVS-Lip基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件驱动唇读方法通常在进行充分的时间建模之前就执行空间压缩，这可能抑制了区分相似唇部运动所需的关键稀疏局部运动轨迹；此外，大多数方法主要在词分类层面优化时间表示，对底层发音结构约束不足。

Method: 1) 轨迹感知差分聚合（TDA）：在每个空间位置进行局部时间建模，再进行自适应空间聚合；2) 视素引导聚合（VGA）：由CTC解码器和视素引导门控聚合分支组成的统一时间模块，注入视素感知的序列监督；3) EMA教师-学生训练策略：增强在强事件扰动下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在DVS-Lip基准上验证了所提设计的有效性；消融实验进一步证实了TDA、VGA和教师-学生一致性各自的贡献；定性解码结果表明基于CTC的时间建模能够从事件流中学习到有意义的视素感知结构。

Conclusion: 本文提出的时间增强框架有效解决了事件驱动唇读中时空建模不平衡的问题，证明了视素级监督和局部时间建模在事件流唇读任务中的重要性。

Abstract: Event-based lip reading has recently emerged as a promising direction for visual speech recognition, benefiting from the high temporal resolution and motion sensitivity of event cameras. However, existing methods typically perform spatial compression before sufficient temporal modeling, which may suppress sparse and localized motion trajectories that are crucial for distinguishing similar lip movements. Moreover, most current approaches optimize temporal representations mainly at the word-classification level, leaving the underlying articulatory structure weakly constrained. To address these limitations, we propose a temporally enhanced framework for event-based lip reading. First, we introduce Trajectory-Aware Differential Aggregation (TDA), which performs local temporal modeling at each spatial location before adaptive spatial aggregation. Second, we propose Viseme-Guided Aggregation (VGA), a unified temporal module composed of a CTC decoder and a viseme-guided gated aggregation branch, which injects viseme-aware sequence supervision and improves final temporal aggregation for word recognition. Third, we incorporate an EMA teacher--student training strategy to enhance robustness under strong event perturbations. Experiments on the DVS-Lip benchmark verify the effectiveness of the proposed design, and extensive ablation studies further validate the contributions of TDA, VGA, and teacher--student consistency. Qualitative decoding results also demonstrate that the proposed CTC-based temporal modeling learns meaningful viseme-aware structure from event streams.

</details>


### [34] [Closing the Null Space: Guidance-Aware Quantization for Classifier-Free Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08241)
*Abdullah Al Shafi,Sumaiya Rahim Suma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) for classifier-free guidance (CFG) diffusion models ignores the paired conditional/unconditional structure, causing system latency overhead and a "branch-drift trap" where unconditional branch drifts corrupting all guided predictions. To close this trap, the paper proposes Guidance-Aware Mixed Precision (GAMP), which calibrates directly on guided predictions, derives per-layer activation-bit sensitivity, and allocates bits via a greedy knapsack, provably preventing unconditional branch drift.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current PTQ methods treat CFG models as single-branch networks, failing to exploit the two-pass conditional/unconditional structure. This leads to: (1) system-level latency overhead masked by BOPs metrics, and (2) algorithmic-level "branch-drift trap" where calibrating on guidance gap alone allows unconditional branch to drift arbitrarily, corrupting all guided predictions.

Method: The paper proposes Guidance-Aware Mixed Precision (GAMP), which: calibrates directly on the guided prediction (not just the gap), derives per-layer activation-bit sensitivity from guided-output degradation, and allocates bits via a greedy knapsack algorithm, provably preventing unconditional branch drift by construction.

Result: The paper analytically proves the existence of the branch-drift trap and empirically confirms it via a false-positive: the best-calibrated model by standard diagnostics produces the worst sample quality. GAMP closes the trap and enables efficient quantization under real-world compute budgets.

Conclusion: Guidance-Aware Mixed Precision (GAMP) effectively addresses the structural blind spot in quantizing CFG diffusion models, preventing unconditional branch drift and achieving better sample quality while enabling the latency benefits of INT8 inference stacks.

Abstract: Deploying classifier-free guidance (CFG) diffusion models under real-world compute budgets requires quantization, yet existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods treat CFG models as single-branch networks, ignoring the paired conditional/unconditional structure that CFG inference fundamentally relies on. This structural blind spot has two consequences. At the system level, the two-pass CFG execution pattern imposes a latency overhead that parameter-count and bit-operation metrics conceal entirely, and commodity INT8 inference stacks fail to realize the theoretical efficiency gains that BOPs calculations promise. At the algorithmic level, calibrating against the guidance gap alone admits an exact null space: a quantized model can achieve perfect gap-fidelity diagnostics while the unconditional branch drifts arbitrarily, corrupting every guided prediction at inference time.
